### Task HR-4.5.R86 — R86 remediation: absolute run-start pre-final watchdog, boundary regressions for base-crossing and mid-flight load cut [M]
- Date: 2026-09-03
- Implementer model: z-ai/glm-5.3-flash
- Status: done (implementation with focused tests on branch `sprint-2/durable-local-index`, HEAD `093ff38` worktree. Task HR-4.5 remains **Blocked** on its four inherited Sprint 3 gates. No release claim, no Deep-as-default change, no commit.)
- Scope: Mechanical R86 correction only, confined to the Deep retrieval agent plus its tests. **Absolute run-start pre-final watchdog (no delayed relative arming):** R84 armed the `pre_final` watchdog only after the initial Fast base pass with the relative delay `(preparation_budget - final_revalidation_reserve) - elapsed` - a base pass that overran the boundary pushed the Deep-only cutoff past run start + (30s - reserve), letting Deep-only work eat into the reserved slice. R86 spawns the watchdog AT RUN START with the absolute delay `preparation_budget - final_revalidation_reserve` (saturating), so the boundary is exactly run start + (30s - reserve) regardless of base-pass duration. Semantics preserved: the initial Fast/base retrieval and hydration still carry the FULL `budget` token and may cross the pre-final instant and even the total envelope (typed `BudgetExhausted`); every Deep-only action after the base pass still carries `pre_final`/its children (R82 threading) and all in-loop guards still test `pre_final.is_cancelled()`, so after a base pass that crossed the boundary the planner loop stops immediately and NO Deep-only action can begin or continue past the cutoff. The fence still runs on `budget` (absolute 30-second cap, no fresh post-expiry token) with R80's reserve-zone target selection (initial Fast/base evidence revalidated), R84's hard post-fence and post-handoff checks (no successful result after 30s), R84's post-prompt zero-deadline re-check, R78's shared open-admission budget, and R53-R85 user-cancellation/typing/source-parity invariants. No new client or registry.
- Files changed: `frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs` (pre-final watchdog spawned at run start with the absolute delay; R84 post-base arming block removed), `frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs` (two NEW boundary regressions), this doc.
- Exact boundary flow (R86): run start: `budget` watchdog at 30s; `pre_final` watchdog at 30s - reserve (both spawned before any work). Initial retrieval + initial hydration on `budget` - may cross the pre-final instant (token fire is a no-op for them) and the total envelope (`initial_failure` -> `BudgetExhausted`). Loop top: `pre_final.is_cancelled()` -> break (a base pass that crossed the boundary never reaches the planner); post-prompt re-check: `remaining <= final_revalidation_reserve` -> break before `generate`. Every Deep-only op awaits `pre_final`/its children: begun inside the cutoff and stopped AT it. Fence: reserve-zone -> initial Fast/base evidence on `budget`; hard post-fence and post-handoff checks keep any result from leaving after 30s.
- Tests (deterministic, hermetic, no network):
  - NEW `base_pass_crossing_the_pre_final_boundary_never_starts_deep_only_work` - the base pass is gated on the pool's single connection and released AFTER the pre-final instant (650ms of an 800ms budget with a 150ms reserve), so the base pass crosses the boundary while gated: the planner loop stops immediately (`planner_round_trips == 0`, `additional_rounds == 0`), the reserve-zone fence re-validates the Fast/base evidence, the handoff runs, and the whole run completes inside the ORIGINAL 800ms deadline - no delayed-watchdog drift, no Deep-only work past the cutoff.
  - NEW `head_load_that_started_before_the_boundary_stops_at_it` - the planner hook acquires the pool's only connection during round-1 generation and releases it well after the pre-final instant (650ms), so the explicit-open head-load STARTS inside the cutoff (direct `HEAD_LOAD_STARTS` instrumentation == 1) and is cut mid-flight by the pre-final watchdog: no `deep:t-m-open` evidence, the round aborts, and the fence re-validates the Fast/base evidence inside the envelope (`elapsed < 800ms`, `additional_rounds == 0`, `AnswerGeneration` reached).
  - All prior deadline tests remain green unchanged: `per_call_deadline_and_total_budget_fall_back_in_time`, `total_envelope_exhaustion_fails_closed_without_publication`, `budget_expiry_during_the_answer_handoff_fails_closed`, `deep_only_work_stops_at_the_pre_final_boundary_and_fits_the_original_deadline`, `explicit_and_range_free_opens_share_one_admission_budget`, plus the user-cancel/zero-budget/initial-failure pins.
- Verification (repo root; `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR = "C:\Users\arman\cargo-target"`):

  - `rustfmt --edition 2021 --check` on the touched Rust files - checkmark clean; `git diff --check` - checkmark (only the pre-existing CRLF->LF notice for `frontend/src/types/index.ts`). The excluded evaluation suite was intentionally NOT run.
- Notes/decisions:
  1. **Run-start spawn vs post-base arming.** The watchdog spawn (not the token creation) was R84's defect: arming after the base pass measured the delay from the arming instant, so a slow base pass pushed the cutoff later. Spawning at run start with the absolute delay fixes the boundary without touching the R84 semantics: the initial pass keeps the full budget token (it is the fallback evidence source and may cross both the pre-final instant and the envelope), and Deep-only work after it finds the token already cancelled.
  2. **Boundary-test staging.** Both regressions gate the base pass / head-load on the pool's single connection (the established `cancelled_sql_load` starvation seam) with releases strictly AFTER the pre-final instant, so token fire, cut, and fence resume are ordered deterministically; fence hydration then runs in the remaining envelope with margins widened (800ms/150ms budgets) after an initial full-suite run exposed a too-tight window under parallel load.

### Review R81 — after Task HR-4.5.R80
- Date: 2026-09-03
- Reviewer model: openai/gpt-5.6-sol
- Verdict: changes-requested
- Findings:
  1. **Blocker — the reserve is carved out only from planner generation, not from the rest of Deep-only round work, so it is still not guaranteed for final revalidation.** The planner call now uses `remaining.saturating_sub(final_revalidation_reserve)` (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:629-655`), but every subsequent additional query, explicit/range-free load, scope recheck, ranking, and round hydration still runs on the full 30-second `budget` token (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:697-975`). Any of those operations can begin just before the reserve boundary and consume the slice until the hard token expires; the code then selects the Fast base only after the round returns and runs its fence on an already-cancelled token (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:993-1029`), producing `BudgetExhausted` instead of the required revalidated-Fast handoff even though the five seconds were nominally reserved. Use a separate Deep-work child token/deadline at `preparation_budget - reserve` for ALL planner/additional/re-rank/round-hydration work, while retaining the single absolute budget token for initial/final validation. The same boundary needs a no-late-call guard: prompt construction occurs between the round-top reserve check and recomputation, so `saturating_sub` can yield a zero call deadline and still invoke `planner.generate` (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:609-655`). Break before generation when no Deep-work time remains. Add a deterministic test where additional work, not planner generation, reaches the reserve boundary and Fast revalidation still succeeds.
  2. **Blocker — successful final hydration can still publish after the hard envelope has expired.** After the fence succeeds, only the parent/user token is checked; the hard budget is never rechecked before or after the synchronous progress handoff (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:1032-1069`). The retained regression explicitly blocks for 1.2 seconds under a 400 ms preparation budget and expects successful evidence publication (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:2145-2187`). That contradicts the architecture's 30-second TOTAL Deep-preparation limit (`docs/hybrid-rag/architecture.md:1484-1502`) and the R80 claim that the envelope is absolute. Keep user cancellation distinct, but if the absolute deadline is spent before return, fail closed without returning/publishing the preparation; make the progress sink non-blocking/bounded if necessary rather than exempting it from the total.
  3. **Should-fix — the requested mixed same-round and loader-boundary proof is still absent.** The test remains range-free-only in round one and explicit-only in round two (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:858-883`), so it does not exercise explicit opens seeding the range-free counter in the same action. It also still infers "loader never started" from missing final `deep:` evidence/markdown (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:913-950`), which is downstream of merge/ranking/final hydration and is exactly the publication coincidence the review asked not to rely on. The production branch itself skips before the head loader (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:831-851`), but the evidence is not falsifiable at that boundary. Add one planner action containing both explicit and range-free IDs and a test-only loader seam/counter (or equivalent direct invocation observation) that records starts before repository I/O; assert five distinct starts in that round, eight total, and zero starts for excess IDs.
  4. **Blocker (release evidence) — Task HR-4.5 and Sprint 4 remain Blocked on all four inherited gates.** The reviewed tree still lacks a valid independently authored Portuguese corpus, production-path quality/final provider-answer evidence, native Windows/R13 hermetic evidence, and exact-head GitHub Actions evidence (`docs/hybrid-rag/sprint-4-deep-saved-scopes.md:74-88`). Unit/component tests and excluded corpus-free diagnostics cannot close them.

Excluded evaluation/corpus/debug/`.opencode` artifacts were not inspected or run.
- Residual risks: R80 is confined to the Deep agent/tests and does not modify R53-R75 source/deletion/ChatPanel code. Full Rust/frontend regressions remain green for transactional source sanitation, deletion-driven request invalidation, terminal publication fences, identity-only post-commit notification, source-free listener degradation, bounded deletion retention, stale-stream isolation, and unmount cleanup. The single hard budget token removes R78's fresh-token 30+5-second path, final hydration remains authoritative and source/prompt parity-safe, envelope exhaustion during the fence fails closed, and exercised parent cancellation remains typed; the remaining defects are incomplete reserve ownership, post-fence wall-clock publication, and non-falsifying mixed/loader tests.

### Review R83 — after Task HR-4.5.R82
- Date: 2026-09-03
- Reviewer model: openai/gpt-5.6-sol
- Verdict: changes-requested
- Findings:
  1. **Blocker — successful Deep preparation can still return after the absolute 30-second envelope.** After final hydration succeeds, the implementation checks only the parent/user token and deliberately omits both the budget token and elapsed deadline before and after the synchronous `AnswerGeneration` callback (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:1052-1089`). The retained regression blocks that callback for 1.2 seconds under a 400 ms preparation budget and requires `Ok` (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:2221-2263`). This directly contradicts the architecture's **30 seconds total Deep preparation** MUST (`docs/hybrid-rag/architecture.md:1500-1502`) and leaves the R81 hard post-fence requirement unresolved. Restore hard elapsed/deadline checks after final hydration and after the callback, preserving parent cancellation as `Cancelled` and mapping total expiry to fail-closed `BudgetExhausted`; make the progress callback non-blocking/bounded if production handoff must not consume the envelope.
  2. **Blocker — the pre-final reserve now shortens the initial Fast/base pass instead of governing only Deep-only work.** The initial retrieval request and initial hydration both carry `pre_final` (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:516-569`), and expiry there is classified as `BudgetExhausted` (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:1092-1113`), which the Chat adapters surface as a preparation failure (`frontend/src-tauri/src/api/chat.rs:1253-1267`, `:1367-1381`). Consequently, a normal Fast-equivalent initial pass that would finish between 25 and 30 seconds is cancelled early and cannot become the required fallback, despite the Sprint contract that non-cancellation Deep failure continues from current Fast evidence (`docs/hybrid-rag/sprint-4-deep-saved-scopes.md:92-104`) and R81's explicit direction to retain the total-budget token for initial/final validation (`docs/notes-chat-improvement-execution.md:3050`). Keep initial retrieval/hydration on the original total budget; apply `pre_final` only after the initial Fast snapshot exists.
  3. **Should-fix — the pre-final boundary still has a late planner-invocation race and its new regression exercises planner timeout, not additional Deep work.** Prompt construction occurs after the round-top boundary check, then `remaining.saturating_sub(reserve)` may become zero and the code still increments the round-trip count and calls `planner.generate` with a zero deadline/cancelled child (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:603-669`). The new deadline test blocks only `DeadlineUnblockingPlanner` (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:803-837`), so it would remain green if additional retrieval/load/rank/hydration ignored the pre-final boundary. Recheck `pre_final`/remaining immediately before generation and add the requested deterministic case where an additional operation reaches the boundary, then prove Fast revalidation completes inside the original envelope.
  4. **Should-fix — direct loader-start instrumentation is present, but the action test is still not mixed in one round.** Round one contains only range-free expansions and round two only explicit opens (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:920-929`); the exact eight-start assertion (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:1010-1013`) proves the total loader boundary but not that explicit opens seed the range-free per-round counter in the same admitted action. Put both `openMeetingIds` and range-free `expandEvidenceIds` in one planner response and assert five starts for that round and eight total.
  5. **Blocker (release evidence) — Task HR-4.5 and Sprint 4 remain Blocked on all four inherited gates.** The tree still lacks a valid independently authored Portuguese corpus, production-path quality/final provider-answer evidence, native Windows/R13 hermetic-session evidence, and exact-head GitHub Actions evidence (`docs/hybrid-rag/sprint-4-deep-saved-scopes.md:74-88`). Unit/component tests cannot substitute for these artifacts.

### Review R85 — after Task HR-4.5.R84
- Date: 2026-09-03

- Findings:
  1. **Blocker — the pre-final watchdog is still relative to when its spawned task is first polled, not the absolute run-start deadline.** `spawn_deadline_watchdog` constructs `tokio::time::sleep(after)` inside the spawned future (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:465-475`). R84 computes a remaining duration from `started.elapsed()` and then spawns that future after the base pass (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:565-574`), so executor delay before the watchdog first runs is added to `run_start + budget - reserve`. Subsequent additional retrieval/load/revalidation/rank/hydration admission relies on `pre_final.is_cancelled()` rather than the absolute elapsed boundary (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:709-744`, `:786-873`, `:904-960`). Under runtime starvation or a slow non-yielding planner poll, Deep-only work can therefore enter and consume the reserved slice, recreating the failure mode R82 was meant to remove. Pass one absolute `Instant` to a `sleep_until` watchdog (and cancel synchronously when arming after that instant), or equivalently enforce the same absolute elapsed deadline at every Deep-work admission boundary. The total watchdog has the same relative-start weakness (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:488-499`), although R84's explicit elapsed checks now correctly prevent an eventual successful return after the total envelope.
  2. **Should-fix — R84's new timing behavior lacks the falsifying regressions requested by R83.** The retained boundary test still waits only for planner-child cancellation (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs:803-837`); there is no test where the initial Fast pass crosses `budget - reserve` but succeeds under the full budget, no callback/prompt-boundary test proving `planner.generate` is never invoked once the absolute pre-final instant has passed, and no additional retrieval/load/rank/hydration operation that reaches that boundary. The post-prompt check is correctly ordered and mathematically prevents a zero-duration call in the ordinary path (`frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs:623-656`), but the suite would not fail if the initial token regressed or later Deep-only operations stopped honoring the intended absolute instant. ### Task HR-4.5.R87 — R87 remediation: absolute sleep_until watchdogs, once-derived cutoff instants, absolute admission predicates, pre-final removal from initial-failure classification, isolated loader-boundary staging [M]
- Date: 2026-09-03
- Implementer model: z-ai/glm-5.3-flash
- Status: done (implementation with focused tests on branch `sprint-2/durable-local-index`, HEAD `093ff38` worktree. Task HR-4.5 remains **Blocked** on its four inherited Sprint 3 gates. No release claim, no Deep-as-default change, no commit.)
- Scope: Mechanical R87 correction only, confined to the Deep retrieval agent plus its tests. (1) **Absolute timers:** `spawn_deadline_watchdog` now takes an ABSOLUTE `tokio::time::Instant` and `sleep_until`s it - both cutoffs are derived ONCE at run start (`budget_deadline = started + preparation_budget`; `pre_final_deadline = started + preparation_budget - final_revalidation_reserve`, saturating) and both watchdogs are spawned there, so delayed task polling can never shift either cutoff. The per-planner-call watchdog also uses an absolute instant (`now + call_deadline`). (2) **Classification:** `initial_failure(parent, budget, error)` no longer takes the pre-final token - a base pass that crossed the boundary can never reclassify an unrelated initial retrieval/database failure as `BudgetExhausted`; precedence stays parent cancellation -> total budget expiry -> typed cancellation -> underlying error. (3) **Absolute admission/await enforcement:** two predicates (`deep_cutoff_passed(pre_final_deadline)`, `envelope_passed(budget_deadline)`) are checked at the loop top, after prompt construction, after action admission, before each additional retrieval/head-load/expansion, and in the post-generation discard check (`deep_cutoff_passed` discards a planner result that raced the cutoff); the planner child watchdog (absolute `now + call_deadline` on a `pre_final` child) plus the token carried by every Deep-only await stop any operation that began inside the cutoff. The final fence and handoff remain on `budget` inside the total deadline, fail closed, and R84's hard post-fence/post-handoff checks now use `envelope_passed(budget_deadline)` - no successful result is published after 30s. R84's full-budget initial pass, R84's post-prompt re-check, R86's run-start watchdog spawn, the R78 shared open-admission budget, and the direct `HEAD_LOAD_STARTS` instrumentation are all preserved; no new client or registry.
- Files changed: `frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs` (absolute watchdog, once-derived cutoff instants, admission predicates, planner-result discard, `initial_failure` signature, stale `BudgetExhausted` doc), `frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs` (classification unit test updated; base-crossing staging widened; NEW `initial_failure_after_crossing_the_boundary_keeps_the_underlying_error`, `planner_never_starts_once_the_pre_final_boundary_has_passed`, `deep_stage_blocked_across_the_boundary_cannot_continue_past_it`; `head_load_that_started_before_the_boundary_stops_at_it` made isolated-deterministic with Fast-fallback assertions), this doc.
- Exact deadline primitives/operation fences: run start derives `budget_deadline`/`pre_final_deadline` and spawns both watchdogs (`sleep_until`). Initial retrieval + initial hydration: full `budget` token only (may cross both instants; failure classified by `initial_failure`). Planner round: loop-top `pre_final.is_cancelled() || deep_cutoff_passed` -> break; prompt built; post-prompt `pre_final.is_cancelled() || deep_cutoff_passed || remaining <= reserve` -> break before `generate`; `generate` on a `pre_final` child with an absolute per-call watchdog `now + min(call_timeout, remaining - reserve)`; post-generate `child.is_cancelled() || deep_cutoff_passed` discards a raced result. Additional retrieval, head-loads, revalidation, ranking, round hydration: admission guard `pre_final.is_cancelled() || deep_cutoff_passed` before each op; the `pre_final` token stops any in-flight op at the cutoff. Fence: on `budget` (absolute 30s cap), reserve-zone target = initial Fast/base evidence; failure with expired envelope -> `BudgetExhausted`; post-fence `envelope_passed` -> `BudgetExhausted` (no publication after 30s); post-handoff `envelope_passed` -> `BudgetExhausted`; parent cancel -> `Cancelled` at every check.
- Tests (deterministic, hermetic, no network):
  - NEW `initial_failure_after_crossing_the_boundary_keeps_the_underlying_error` - the gated base pass crosses the 1600ms boundary of a 2000ms/400ms envelope; a `Meeting("ghost")` scope then fails the initial retrieval genuinely: the surfaced error is `InitialRetrieval(_)`, never `BudgetExhausted`.
  - NEW `planner_never_starts_once_the_pre_final_boundary_has_passed` - base pass gated past the 800ms boundary of a 1500ms/700ms envelope: the planner never starts (`planner_round_trips == 0`), the reserve-zone fence re-validates the Fast/base evidence, and the run completes inside the original 1500ms deadline.
  - NEW `deep_stage_blocked_across_the_boundary_cannot_continue_past_it` - budget 300ms/reserve 100ms: the round-1 head-load starts inside the cutoff (`HEAD_LOAD_STARTS == 1`) and is blocked on the pool's held connection across the 200ms boundary; once the connection returns the stage's own cancellation checks reject it, the round aborts, and the spent envelope fails closed (`BudgetExhausted`, no `AnswerGeneration`, wall clock within budget + slack).
  - `head_load_that_started_before_the_boundary_stops_at_it` (R86 test made isolated-deterministic per R87) - the release is scheduled at the ABSOLUTE instant `cutoff + 50ms` (`sleep_until`), so the loader is always blocked across the cutoff regardless of hook drift; assertions assert the FAST FALLBACK outcome: `HEAD_LOAD_STARTS == 1`, zero `deep:` evidence of any kind, run completes inside the 2000ms envelope, handoff reached - not presence/absence of the loaded content.
  - `initial_failures_map_typed_cancellation_by_variant` updated (pre-final case removed - the boundary never reclassifies); `base_pass_crossing...` wording corrected (1600ms boundary, 2000ms envelope); all other deadline/mixed-action tests unchanged green.
- Verification (repo root; `$env:CARGO_TARGET_DIR = "C:\Users\arman\cargo-target"`):
  - `cargo test --manifest-path "frontend/src-tauri/Cargo.toml" --lib` - checkmark 843 passed / 0 failed / 2 ignored (was 840: net +3), stable across repeated full-suite runs after widening the gated-test envelopes (an initial tight-margin flake in two gated tests was diagnosed and fixed by enlarging budget/reserve windows, not by loosening assertions).
  - `cargo check --manifest-path "src-tauri/Cargo.toml"` - checkmark with only the documented pre-existing `Sidebar` dead-code warning.
  - `pnpm run typecheck` - checkmark (`tsc --noEmit` clean); `npx vitest run` - checkmark 112 passed / 20 files (frontend untouched this round).
  - Isolated deadline tests - checkmark `cargo test --lib retrieval::agent::tests::` focused runs green for all boundary/staging tests.
  - `rustfmt --edition 2021 --check` on the touched Rust files - checkmark clean; `git diff --check` - checkmark (only the pre-existing CRLF->LF notice for `frontend/src/types/index.ts`). The excluded evaluation suite was intentionally NOT run.
- Notes/decisions:
  1. **Absolute over relative.** Both watchdogs are spawned at run start with `sleep_until` on instants derived once; a relative post-base arming (R84) let a slow base pass push the Deep-only cutoff later. The initial pass keeps the full token by design, so crossing the pre-final instant is expected and harmless for it.
  2. **Discard, not drop.** The planner generation future is not dropped at the cutoff (that would skip the child-token shutdown path the BuiltInAI/remote sidecars rely on); instead its child watchdog fires at `now + call_deadline <= pre_final_deadline`, and the post-generate `deep_cutoff_passed`/`child.is_cancelled` checks discard the raced result before any action is admitted.
  3. **Blocked-across-boundary staging.** The service checks cancellation BETWEEN its SQL stages, so a Deep-only op blocked on a held pool connection deterministically observes the fired token at its next internal check once the connection returns - the staging proves the stage cannot continue past the boundary without needing a hook inside the service.
- Inherited Sprint 3 release gates (unchanged, each still absent): valid independently authored Portuguese corpus; production-path quality and final provider-answer evidence; native Windows/R13 hermetic session evidence; exact-head GitHub Actions evidence. Deep latency/round-trip/quality metrics and native smoke remain unmeasured/unrun; nothing unrun is reported as passed.
- Spillover: none. No rejected corpus/evaluation/debug file was read, edited, staged, or run; no commit was made.

### Task HR-4.5.R89 — R89 remediation of the R88 finding: absolute pre-final re-check after every synchronous progress callback in all Deep additional-search/open/expand paths [M]
- Date: 2026-09-03

- Status: done (implementation with focused tests on branch `sprint-2/durable-local-index`, HEAD `093ff38` worktree. Task HR-4.5 remains **Blocked** on its four inherited Sprint 3 gates - R88 review finding 2, unchanged. No release claim, no Deep-as-default change, no commit.)
- Scope: Mechanical R88-finding-1 correction only, confined to the Deep retrieval agent plus its tests. In all three additional-work paths - additional queries, explicit opens, and range-free/neighborhood expansions - the existing `pre_final.is_cancelled() || deep_cutoff_passed(pre_final_deadline)` predicate is now REPEATED immediately AFTER each pre-operation `emit_progress` callback and BEFORE any query-slot mutation, `HEAD_LOAD_STARTS` instrumentation, repository/loader invocation, or await. The callback is synchronous caller code (production enters the Chat ownership/Tauri publication path), so scheduler delay or a blocked callback can cross the once-derived pre-final instant after admission; the repeated predicate closes that bypass with the smallest repeated check - no new timer, event bus, registry, or client, and no change to the R53-R87 contracts (absolute once-derived instants, `sleep_until` watchdogs, full-budget initial pass, planner race/discard, fence on the absolute budget, R84 hard post-fence/post-handoff checks, R78 shared open admission). After the re-check breaks, the round flows into the existing paths: scope revalidation observes the fired token and aborts the round, and the fence takes the reserve-zone revalidated-Fast-evidence fallback (or fails closed if the envelope is spent).
- Exact callback-to-admission order (each of the three paths): admission guards (parent/pre-final predicate) -> `emit_progress` (synchronous caller callback) -> **R88 re-check (`pre_final.is_cancelled() || deep_cutoff_passed(pre_final_deadline)` -> break)** -> query-slot mutation / `HEAD_LOAD_STARTS` instrumentation / repository-loader invocation / await (each await carries `pre_final`, stopping in-flight work at the boundary).
- Files changed: `frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent.rs` (three post-progress re-checks), `frontend/src-tauri/src/retrieval/agent/tests.rs` (NEW blocking-callback regression), this doc.
